EXAM MASTER QUESTION BANK — 2000 MCQs
Hindi + English | A/B/C/D | Answer | Short Solution | Quick Revision

IMPORTANT SOURCE STATUS:
• Q1–35: Core practice questions based on standard syllabus; not claimed as exact PYQ.
• Q36–2000: Pattern-Based Practice generated from common exam concepts. They are NOT claimed as verbatim previous-year questions.
• For exact PYQ preparation, official papers/memory-based papers should be cross-checked separately.

### Electrical & Electronics — Q1 to Q300

Q1. ओम के नियम के अनुसार धारा I का सूत्र क्या है?
English: According to Ohm's law, what is the formula for current I?
A. I=VR
B. I=V/R
C. I=R/V
D. I=V+R
Answer: B
Solution: Ohm's law: V=IR, इसलिए I=V/R.
Quick Revision: Q1 → B

Q2. विद्युत प्रतिरोध की SI इकाई क्या है?
English: What is the SI unit of electrical resistance?
A. Volt
B. Ampere
C. Ohm
D. Watt
Answer: C
Solution: Resistance की SI unit Ohm (Ω) है.
Quick Revision: Q2 → C

Q3. विद्युत शक्ति का सामान्य DC सूत्र क्या है?
English: What is the basic DC formula for electrical power?
A. P=VI
B. P=V/I
C. P=I/V
D. P=V+I
Answer: A
Solution: Electrical power P=VI.
Quick Revision: Q3 → A

Q4. भारत में AC supply की सामान्य frequency कितनी है?
English: What is the standard AC supply frequency in India?
A. 25 Hz
B. 40 Hz
C. 50 Hz
D. 60 Hz
Answer: C
Solution: भारत की standard AC frequency 50 Hz है.
Quick Revision: Q4 → C

Q5. Ammeter को circuit में कैसे जोड़ा जाता है?
English: How is an ammeter connected in a circuit?
A. Parallel
B. Series
C. Open circuit
D. Across supply only
Answer: B
Solution: Ammeter को series में जोड़ा जाता है.
Quick Revision: Q5 → B

Q6. Voltmeter को circuit में कैसे जोड़ा जाता है?
English: How is a voltmeter connected in a circuit?
A. Series
B. Parallel
C. Short circuit
D. In series with fuse
Answer: B
Solution: Voltage measure करने के लिए voltmeter parallel में लगाया जाता है.
Quick Revision: Q6 → B

Q7. Transformer किस principle पर काम करता है?
English: On which principle does a transformer operate?
A. Electrolysis
B. Mutual induction
C. Photoelectric effect
D. Heating effect
Answer: B
Solution: Transformer mutual electromagnetic induction पर काम करता है.
Quick Revision: Q7 → B

Q8. Transformer frequency को बदलता है?
English: Does a transformer change frequency?
A. Yes, always
B. No, ideally unchanged
C. Only DC frequency
D. Doubles it
Answer: B
Solution: Transformer voltage/current बदलता है; supply frequency unchanged रहती है.
Quick Revision: Q8 → B

Q9. Power factor का ideal maximum value क्या है?
English: What is the ideal maximum value of power factor?
A. 0
B. 0.5
C. 1
D. 2
Answer: C
Solution: Ideal unity power factor = 1.
Quick Revision: Q9 → C

Q10. DC motor में back EMF का मुख्य प्रभाव क्या है?
English: What is the main effect of back EMF in a DC motor?
A. Limits armature current
B. Increases frequency
C. Stops rotation
D. Converts AC to DC
Answer: A
Solution: Back EMF applied voltage का oppose करके armature current को limit करता है.
Quick Revision: Q10 → A

Q11. Diode का मुख्य कार्य क्या है?
English: What is the basic function of a diode?
A. Bidirectional conduction only
B. Unidirectional current conduction
C. Mechanical switching
D. Voltage generation
Answer: B
Solution: Ideal diode current को मुख्यतः एक दिशा में conduct करता है.
Quick Revision: Q11 → B

Q12. Rectifier क्या करता है?
English: What does a rectifier do?
A. DC to AC
B. AC to DC
C. AC to AC only
D. DC to DC only
Answer: B
Solution: Rectifier AC को pulsating DC में convert करता है.
Quick Revision: Q12 → B

Q13. Zener diode का प्रमुख उपयोग क्या है?
English: What is a major use of a Zener diode?
A. Voltage regulation
B. Mechanical drive
C. Frequency counting
D. Heating
Answer: A
Solution: Zener diode voltage regulation/reference के लिए widely used है.
Quick Revision: Q13 → A

Q14. BJT का full form क्या है?
English: What is the full form of BJT?
A. Bipolar Junction Transistor
B. Basic Junction Terminal
C. Binary Junction Transformer
D. Bipolar Joint Terminal
Answer: A
Solution: BJT = Bipolar Junction Transistor.
Quick Revision: Q14 → A

Q15. MOSFET के तीन मुख्य terminals कौन से हैं?
English: What are the three main MOSFET terminals?
A. Gate, Source, Drain
B. Base, Emitter, Collector
C. Anode, Cathode, Gate
D. Line, Neutral, Earth
Answer: A
Solution: MOSFET terminals: Gate, Source और Drain.
Quick Revision: Q15 → A

Q16. IPv4 address कितने bits का होता है?

A. 16
B. 32
C. 64
D. 128
Answer: B
Solution: IPv4 = 32-bit addressing.
Quick Revision: Q16 → B

B. 64

A. Central Processing Unit
B. Computer Primary Unit
C. Central Program Utility
D. Control Processing User
Answer: A
Solution: CPU = Central Processing Unit.

Q19. RAM किस प्रकार की memory है?
English: What type of memory is RAM?
A. Volatile
B. Permanent ROM
C. Optical
D. Magnetic tape only
Answer: A
Solution: RAM volatile memory है; power off होने पर data सामान्यतः खो जाता है.
Quick Revision: Q19 → A

Q22. 25 का 20% कितना है?
English: What is 20% of 25?
A. 2
B. 4
C. 5
D. 10
Answer: C
Solution: 25 × 20/100 = 5.
Quick Revision: Q22 → C

Q23. यदि 5x=45 है तो x कितना होगा?
English: If 5x=45, what is x?
A. 5
B. 7
C. 8
D. 9
Answer: D
Solution: x=45/5=9.
Quick Revision: Q23 → D

Q24. श्रृंखला 2,4,8,16,? में अगली संख्या क्या है?
English: What is the next number in 2,4,8,16,?
A. 20
B. 24
C. 32
D. 36
Answer: C
Solution: हर term ×2 है; next = 32.
Quick Revision: Q24 → C

Q25. CAT को DBU लिखा जाए तो DOG को कैसे लिखा जाएगा?
English: If CAT is coded as DBU, how will DOG be coded?
A. EPH
B. EOG
C. FPH
D. DNF
Answer: A
Solution: हर letter को alphabet में +1 किया गया है: D→E, O→P, G→H.
Quick Revision: Q25 → A

Q26. मध्य प्रदेश की राजधानी क्या है?

A. Indore
B. Bhopal
C. Jabalpur
D. Gwalior
Answer: B
Solution: मध्य प्रदेश की राजधानी Bhopal है.
Quick Revision: Q26 → B

B. Indore
C. Panna
D. Dhar
Answer: A
Solution: Sanchi, Raisen district में स्थित है.
Quick Revision: Q27 → A

A. Rock shelters and prehistoric paintings
B. Modern IT park
C. Sea port
D. Coal refinery

Solution: Bhimbetka prehistoric rock shelters और paintings के लिए प्रसिद्ध है.

Q29. मध्य प्रदेश की प्रमुख नदी कौन-सी है जिसे राज्य की lifeline कहा जाता है?
English: Which major river is often called the lifeline of Madhya Pradesh?
A. Narmada
B. Yamuna
C. Tapi only
D. Mahanadi

Solution: Light speed ≈ 3×10^8 m/s.

Q34. 3-phase AC में phases के बीच phase difference कितना होता है?
English: What is the phase difference between phases in a 3-phase AC system?
A. 60°
B. 90°
C. 120°
D. 180°
Answer: C
Solution: Balanced 3-phase system में phases 120° apart होते हैं.
Quick Revision: Q34 → C

Q35. Synchronous speed का formula क्या है?
English: What is the formula for synchronous speed?
A. Ns=60P/f
B. Ns=120f/P
C. Ns=fP/120
D. Ns=120P/f
Answer: B
Solution: Ns = 120f/P rpm.
Quick Revision: Q35 → B

Q36. Parallel circuit में voltage कैसा रहता है?

B. Always zero
C. Always different
D. Infinite
Answer: A
Solution: Parallel branches same two nodes पर होती हैं, इसलिए voltage same होता है.

### Reasoning — Q301 to Q550

Q301. यदि सभी A, B हैं और सभी B, C हैं, तो A का C से क्या संबंध है?
English: If all A are B and all B are C, what follows about A and C?
A. All A are C
B. No A is C
C. Some C are not B
D. None
Answer: A
Solution: Transitive relation: A ⊂ B ⊂ C, इसलिए A ⊂ C.
Quick Revision: Q301 → A

Q302. Odd one out चुनिए: 2, 3, 5, 9
English: Find the odd one out: 2, 3, 5, 9
A. 2
B. 3
C. 5
D. 9
Answer: D
Solution: 2,3,5 prime हैं; 9 composite है.
Quick Revision: Q302 → D

Q303. यदि SOUTH को TPVUI लिखा जाए, तो NORTH कैसे लिखा जाएगा?
English: If SOUTH is coded as TPVUI, how is NORTH coded?
A. OPSUI
B. OPSUI
C. OQSTI
D. NPSUI
Answer: A
Solution: हर letter को +1 shift करने पर NORTH→OPSUI.
Quick Revision: Q303 → A

Q304. एक घड़ी में 3 बजे hour और minute hand के बीच angle कितना है?
English: What is the angle between hour and minute hands at 3 o'clock?
A. 60°
B. 90°
C. 120°
D. 180°
Answer: B
Solution: 3 बजे hands के बीच 90° होता है.
Quick Revision: Q304 → B

### Quantitative Aptitude — Q551 to Q800

Q551. 10 का 25% कितना है?
English: What is 25% of 10?
A. 2.5
B. 7.5
C. 1
D. 12.5
Answer: A
Solution: 10×25/100 = 2.5.
Quick Revision: Q551 → A

Q552. यदि 10x=30 है तो x कितना होगा?
English: If 10x=30, what is x?
A. 2
B. 3
C. 4
D. 5
Answer: B
Solution: x=30/10=3.
Quick Revision: Q552 → B

Q553. यदि 4x=16 है तो x कितना होगा?

A. 3
B. 4
C. 5
D. 6
Answer: B
Solution: x=16/4=4.

Q560. 2Ω और 5Ω resistors series में जुड़े हैं। Equivalent resistance क्या होगी?
English: 2Ω and 5Ω resistors are connected in series. What is the equivalent resistance?
A. 3Ω
B. 7Ω
C. 10Ω
D. 8Ω
Answer: B
Solution: Series resistance = 2+5=7Ω.
Quick Revision: Q560 → B

### Banking & Financial Awareness — Q801 to Q1000

Q801. Repo rate किससे संबंधित है?
English: Repo rate is related to what?
A. RBI lending to banks
B. Tax collection
C. Insurance premium
D. Stock dividend
Answer: A
Solution: Repo rate वह rate है जिस पर RBI eligible securities के विरुद्ध banks को short-term funds देता है.
Quick Revision: Q801 → A

Q802. KYC का full form क्या है?
English: What is the full form of KYC?
A. Know Your Customer
B. Keep Your Cash
C. Know Your Credit
D. Key Yield Control
Answer: A
Solution: KYC = Know Your Customer.
Quick Revision: Q802 → A

B. Tax collection
C. Insurance premium
D. Stock dividend
Answer: A
Solution: Repo rate वह rate है जिस पर RBI eligible securities के विरुद्ध banks को short-term funds देता है.

### MP GK — Q1301 to Q1500

Q1301. मध्य प्रदेश को किस नाम से भी जाना जाता है?
English: What is Madhya Pradesh also commonly known as?
A. Heart of India
B. Land of Five Rivers
C. Spice State
D. Desert State
Answer: A
Solution: MP की central location के कारण इसे Heart of India कहा जाता है.
Quick Revision: Q1301 → A

Q1302. ग्वालियर किस संगीत परंपरा के लिए प्रसिद्ध है?
English: Gwalior is especially associated with which musical tradition?
A. Gwalior gharana
B. Jaipur only
C. Patiala only
D. Kirana only
Answer: A
Solution: Gwalior gharana Hindustani classical music की प्रमुख परंपरा है.
Quick Revision: Q1302 → A

Q1303. मध्य प्रदेश का सबसे बड़ा शहर जनसंख्या की दृष्टि से कौन-सा माना जाता है?
English: Which is generally regarded as the largest city of Madhya Pradesh by population?
A. Indore
B. Sagar
C. Rewa
D. Satna
Answer: A
Solution: Indore राज्य का प्रमुख और सबसे बड़ा urban centre है.
Quick Revision: Q1303 → A

Q1304. तansen किस शहर से विशेष रूप से जुड़े हैं?

C. Rewa
D. Betul
Answer: A

### MP Police / SI Relevant — Q1701 to Q1850

Q1701. FIR का full form क्या है?
English: What is the full form of FIR?
A. First Information Report
B. Final Investigation Record
C. First Inquiry Register
D. Field Incident Report
Answer: A
Solution: FIR = First Information Report.
Quick Revision: Q1701 → A

Q1702. IPC का traditional full form क्या था?
English: What was the traditional full form of IPC?
A. Indian Penal Code
B. Indian Police Code
C. Investigation Procedure Code
D. Indian Public Code
Answer: A
Solution: IPC = Indian Penal Code; criminal law framework has since been replaced by newer criminal codes.
Quick Revision: Q1702 → A

Q1703. CrPC का full form क्या था?
English: What was the full form of CrPC?
A. Code of Criminal Procedure
B. Central Police Regulation Code
C. Criminal Public Record Code
D. Court Procedure Rule Code
Answer: A
Solution: CrPC = Code of Criminal Procedure; criminal procedure law has since been replaced by newer legislation.
Quick Revision: Q1703 → A

Q1704. Evidence का सामान्य अर्थ क्या है?
English: What does evidence generally mean in law?
A. Material/information used to establish facts
B. Police uniform
C. Court building
D. FIR number only
Answer: A
Solution: Evidence facts को prove/disprove करने के लिए relevant material/information होता है.
Quick Revision: Q1704 → A

### JE / Sub Engineer Mixed Technical — Q1851 to Q2000

Q1851. Earthing का मुख्य उद्देश्य क्या है?
English: What is the main purpose of electrical earthing?
A. Provide a safe fault-current path
B. Increase supply frequency
C. Increase load resistance
D. Generate power
Answer: A
Solution: Earthing fault current के लिए low-impedance safe path प्रदान करती है.
Quick Revision: Q1851 → A

Q1852. Megger किस measurement के लिए उपयोग होता है?
English: What is a Megger used to measure?
A. Insulation resistance
B. Frequency only
C. Power factor only
D. Temperature only
Answer: A
Solution: Megger high-value insulation resistance measure करता है.
Quick Revision: Q1852 → A

Q1853. Transformer में laminated core का उद्देश्य क्या है?
English: What is the purpose of a laminated transformer core?
A. Reduce eddy-current losses
B. Increase friction
C. Increase mechanical load
D. Stop magnetic flux
Answer: A
Solution: Lamination eddy-current losses को reduce करती है.
Quick Revision: Q1853 → A

Q1854. MCB का मुख्य उपयोग क्या है?
English: What is the main use of an MCB?
A. Automatic protection against overcurrent/short circuit
B. Measure energy
C. Increase voltage
D. Convert AC to DC
Answer: A
Solution: MCB overcurrent और short-circuit protection देता है.
Quick Revision: Q1854 → A

D. Temperature only
Answer: A
